610:   Future<_EventDetailData> _loadEventDetailData(
611:     DataEventsStruct eventData,
612:   ) async {
613:     EventsRecord? eventRecord;
614:     VenuesRecord? venueRecord;
615: 
616:     final eventRef = eventData.docRef;
617:     if (eventRef != null) {
618:       try {
619:         eventRecord = await EventsRecord.getDocumentOnce(eventRef);
620:       } catch (error) {
621:         debugPrint('Unable to load selected event: $error');
622:       }
623:     }
624: 
625:     final venueRef = eventData.iDVenuse ?? eventRecord?.iDVenues;
626:     if (venueRef != null) {
627:       try {
628:         venueRecord = await VenuesRecord.getDocumentOnce(venueRef);
629:       } catch (error) {
630:         debugPrint('Unable to load event venue: $error');
631:       }
632:     }
633: 
634:     return _EventDetailData(
635:       eventData: eventData,
636:       eventRecord: eventRecord,
637:       venueRecord: venueRecord,
638:     );
639:   }
640: 
641:   Future<void> _showEventDetailSheet(DataEventsStruct? eventData) async {
642:     if (eventData == null || eventData.nameStore == '007') {
643:       return;
644:     }
645: 
646:     final detailFuture = _loadEventDetailData(eventData);
647:     final topSafeInset = MediaQueryData.fromView(
648:       View.of(context),
649:     ).viewPadding.top;
650:     final appState = context.appState;
651:     appState.eventDetailOpen = true;
652: 
653:     try {
654:       // Let the native adaptive nav bar leave the view hierarchy before the
655:       // root modal is presented; otherwise it can remain above Flutter's
656:       // bottom-sheet layer for the first frame on iOS.
657:       await SchedulerBinding.instance.endOfFrame;
658:       if (!mounted) {
659:         return;
660:       }
661: 
662:       await Navigator.of(context, rootNavigator: true).push<void>(
663:         PageRouteBuilder<void>(
664:           opaque: false,
665:           barrierColor: const Color(0xB3000000),
666:           barrierDismissible: true,
667:           transitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
668:           reverseTransitionDuration: const Duration(milliseconds: 300),
669:           pageBuilder: (context, animation, secondaryAnimation) {
670:             return FadeTransition(
671:               opacity: animation,
672:               child: Material(
673:                 type: MaterialType.transparency,
674:                 child: Builder(
675:                   builder: (bottomSheetContext) {
676:           return _EventDetailSheet(
677:             detailFuture: detailFuture,
678:             fallbackEventData: eventData,
679:             topSafeInset: topSafeInset,
680:             onViewVenue: (detailData) {
681:               Navigator.of(bottomSheetContext).pop();
682: 
683:               final venueRef = detailData.venueRef;
684:               if (venueRef == null) {
685:                 return;
686:               }
687: 
688:               context.pushNamed(
689:                 InVenusePage.routeName,
690:                 queryParameters: {
691:                   'idVenues': serializeParam(
692:                     venueRef,
693:                     ParamType.SupabaseDocRef,
694:                   ),
695:                   'distance': serializeParam(
696:                     detailData.distanceValue,
697:                     ParamType.String,
698:                   ),
699:                   'dateclick': serializeParam(
700:                     detailData.eventDate ?? context.appState.dateclick,
701:                     ParamType.DateTime,
702:                   ),
703:                   'index': serializeParam(0, ParamType.int),
704:                 }.withoutNulls,
705:               );
706:             },
707:           );
708:         },
709:                 ),
710:               ),
711:             );
712:           },
713:         ),
714:       );
715:     } finally {
716:       appState.eventDetailOpen = false;
717:       if (mounted) {
718:         safeSetState(() {});
719:       }
720:     }
721:   }

8947: 
8948: class _TopEventRow extends StatelessWidget {
8949:   const _TopEventRow({required this.event, required this.onTap});
8950: 
8951:   final DataEventsStruct event;
8952:   final VoidCallback onTap;
8953: 
8954:   String get _title {
8955:     final artists = event.nameArtise
8956:         .where((artist) => artist.trim().isNotEmpty)
8957:         .join(' × ');
8958:     return artists.isNotEmpty ? artists : event.nameStore;
8959:   }
8960: 
8961:   String get _price {
8962:     if (event.free) return 'FREE';
8963:     final price = event.priceDetail.trim();
8964:     if (price.isEmpty) return 'ดูราคา';
8965:     return RegExp(r'[^0-9.,\s]').hasMatch(price) ? price : '$price ฿';
8966:   }

8969:       ? 'เร็ว ๆ นี้'
8970:       : dateTimeFormat('EEE d MMM', event.date!);

9806: class _FeaturedEventsHero extends StatefulWidget {
9807:   const _FeaturedEventsHero({
9808:     required this.events,
9809:     required this.topInset,
9810:     required this.onPageChanged,
9811:     required this.onEventTap,
9812:   });
9813: 
9814:   final List<EventsRecord> events;
9815:   final double topInset;
9816:   final ValueChanged<int> onPageChanged;
9817:   final ValueChanged<EventsRecord> onEventTap;
9818: 
9819:   @override
9820:   State<_FeaturedEventsHero> createState() => _FeaturedEventsHeroState();
9821: }
9822: 
9823: class _FeaturedEventsHeroState extends State<_FeaturedEventsHero> {
9824:   late final PageController _pageController;
9825:   int _currentIndex = 0;
9826:   bool _muted = true;
9827: 
9828:   @override
9829:   void initState() {
9830:     super.initState();
9831:     _pageController = PageController();
9832:   }
9833: 
9834:   @override
9835:   void didUpdateWidget(covariant _FeaturedEventsHero oldWidget) {
9836:     super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
9837:     if (_currentIndex >= widget.events.length && widget.events.isNotEmpty) {
9838:       _currentIndex = 0;
9839:       WidgetsBinding.instance.addPostFrameCallback((_) {
9840:         if (_pageController.hasClients) _pageController.jumpToPage(0);
9841:       });
9842:     }
9843:   }
9844: 
9845:   @override
9846:   void dispose() {
9847:     _pageController.dispose();
9848:     super.dispose();
9849:   }
9850: 
9851:   @override
9852:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
9853:     if (widget.events.isEmpty) return const SizedBox.shrink();
9854: 
9855:     final safeIndex = _currentIndex.clamp(0, widget.events.length - 1);
9856:     final screenSize = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context);
9857:     final heroHeight = _eventsFeaturedHeroHeight(
9858:       screenSize: screenSize,
9859:       topInset: widget.topInset,
9860:     );
9861: 
9862:     return AnimatedContainer(
9863:       duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 280),
9864:       curve: Curves.easeOutCubic,
9865:       width: double.infinity,
9866:       height: heroHeight,
9867:       child: Stack(
9868:         children: [
9869:           PageView.builder(
9870:             controller: _pageController,
9871:             itemCount: widget.events.length,
9872:             onPageChanged: (index) {
9873:               setState(() {
9874:                 _currentIndex = index;
9875:                 _muted = true;
9876:               });
9877:               widget.onPageChanged(index);
9878:             },
9879:             itemBuilder: (context, index) {
9880:               final event = widget.events[index];
9881:               final videoPath = _featuredVideoPath(event, mockIndex: index);
9882:               if (videoPath != null) {
9883:                 return _buildVideoHero(
9884:                   context,
9885:                   event,
9886:                   videoPath,
9887:                   index == safeIndex,
9888:                 );
9889:               }
9890:               return _buildImageHero(context, event);
9891:             },
9892:           ),
9893:         ],
9894:       ),
9895:     );
9896:   }
9897: 
9898:   Widget _buildVideoHero(
9899:     BuildContext context,
9900:     EventsRecord event,
9901:     String videoPath,
9902:     bool active,
9903:   ) {
9904:     final size = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context);
9905:     final height = _eventsFeaturedHeroHeight(
9906:       screenSize: size,
9907:       topInset: widget.topInset,
9908:     );
9909:     final posterUrl = _isVideoMediaPath(event.poster)
9910:         ? _kEventsFallbackPosterUrl
9911:         : _safeEventsImageUrl(
9912:             event.poster,
9913:             fallback: _kEventsFallbackPosterUrl,
9914:           );
9915: 
9916:     return GestureDetector(
9917:       behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
9918:       onTap: () => widget.onEventTap(event),
9919:       child: Stack(
9920:         fit: StackFit.expand,
9921:         children: [
9922:           Image.network(
9923:             posterUrl,
9924:             fit: BoxFit.cover,
9925:             errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) =>
9926:                 Container(color: const Color(0xFF111217)),
9927:           ),
9928:           MundayVideoPlayer(
9929:             key: ValueKey('$videoPath-$active'),
9930:             path: videoPath,
9931:             videoType: videoPath.startsWith('assets/')
9932:                 ? VideoType.asset
9933:                 : VideoType.network,
9934:             width: size.width,
9935:             height: height,
9936:             aspectRatio: size.width / height,
9937:             autoPlay: active,
9938:             looping: true,
9939:             showControls: false,
9940:             allowFullScreen: false,
9941:             volume: active && !_muted ? 1.0 : 0.0,
9942:           ),
9943:           const Positioned(
9944:             left: 0.0,
9945:             right: 0.0,
9946:             bottom: 0.0,
9947:             height: 150.0,
9948:             child: DecoratedBox(
9949:               decoration: BoxDecoration(
9950:                 gradient: LinearGradient(
9951:                   colors: [Color(0x00000000), Color(0xFF000000)],
9952:                   begin: Alignment.topCenter,
9953:                   end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
9954:                 ),
9955:               ),
9956:             ),
9957:           ),
9958:           Positioned(
9959:             left: 16.0,
9960:             right: 16.0,
9961:             bottom: 18.0,
9962:             child: _FeaturedEventInfoCard(event: event),
9963:           ),
9964:           Positioned(
9965:             left: 14.0,
9966:             top:
9967:                 widget.topInset -
9968:                 _kEventsHeaderHeight +
9969:                 8.0 +
9970:                 _kEventsProfileButtonSize +
9971:                 8.0,
9972:             child: GestureDetector(
9973:               behavior: HitTestBehavior.opaque,
9974:               onTap: () => setState(() => _muted = !_muted),
9975:               child: Container(
9976:                 width: _kEventsProfileButtonSize,
9977:                 height: _kEventsProfileButtonSize,
9978:                 alignment: Alignment.center,
9979:                 child: Icon(
9980:                   _muted ? Icons.volume_off_rounded : Icons.volume_up_rounded,
9981:                   color: Colors.white,
9982:                   size: 26.0,
9983:                 ),
9984:               ),
9985:             ),
9986:           ),
9987:         ],
9988:       ),
9989:     );
9990:   }

10161: class _FeaturedEventInfoCard extends StatelessWidget {
10162:   const _FeaturedEventInfoCard({required this.event});
10163: 
10164:   final EventsRecord event;
10165: 
10166:   String _venueName(VenuesRecord? venue) {
10167:     final venueName = venue?.nameVenuse.trim() ?? '';
10168:     if (venueName.isNotEmpty) return venueName;
10169:     if (event.nameStore.trim().isNotEmpty) return event.nameStore.trim();
10170:     return 'สถานที่จัดงาน';
10171:   }
10172: 
10173:   String get _eventName {
10174:     final artists = event.nameArtise
10175:         .where((artist) => artist.trim().isNotEmpty)
10176:         .join(' • ');
10177:     if (artists.isNotEmpty) return artists;
10178:     return 'Featured event';
10179:   }
10180: 
10181:   String _venueImageUrl(VenuesRecord? venue) {
10182:     final logo = venue?.logo.trim() ?? '';
10183:     if (logo.isNotEmpty) return logo;
10184:     final background = venue?.bg.trim() ?? '';
10185:     return background;
10186:   }
10187: 
10188:   String get _eventDay =>
10189:       event.date == null ? '--' : dateTimeFormat('d', event.date!);
10190: 
10191:   String get _eventMonth => event.date == null
10192:       ? 'เร็ว ๆ นี้'
10193:       : dateTimeFormat('MMM', event.date!).toUpperCase();
10194: 
10195:   @override
10196:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
10197:     final venueRef = event.iDVenues;
10198:     if (venueRef == null) return _buildCard(context, null);
10199: 
10200:       : dateTimeFormat('MMM', event.date!).toUpperCase();
10201: 
10202:   @override
10203:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
10204:     final venueRef = event.iDVenues;
10205:     if (venueRef == null) return _buildCard(context, null);
10206: 
10207:     return StreamBuilder<VenuesRecord>(
10208:       stream: VenuesRecord.getDocument(venueRef),
10209:       builder: (context, snapshot) => _buildCard(context, snapshot.data),
10210:     );
10211:   }
10212:

10220:             width: 54.0,
10221:             height: 54.0,
10222:             child: ClipOval(
10223:               child: venueImageUrl.isEmpty
10224:                   ? const ColoredBox(
10225:                       color: Color(0xFF202027),
10226:                       child: Icon(
10227:                         Icons.storefront_rounded,
10228:                         color: Colors.white70,
10229:                         size: 25.0,
10230:                       ),
10231:                     )
10232:                   : Image.network(
10233:                       _safeEventsImageUrl(
10234:                         venueImageUrl,
10235:                         fallback: _kEventsFallbackProfileUrl,
10236:                       ),

10239:                           const ColoredBox(
10240:                             color: Color(0xFF202027),
10241:                             child: Icon(
10242:                               Icons.storefront_rounded,
10243:                               color: Colors.white70,

10411: class _EventDetailData {
10412:   const _EventDetailData({
10413:     required this.eventData,
10414:     this.eventRecord,
10415:     this.venueRecord,
10416:   });
10417: 
10418:   final DataEventsStruct eventData;
10419:   final EventsRecord? eventRecord;
10420:   final VenuesRecord? venueRecord;
10421: 
10422:   SupabaseDocRef? get venueRef => eventData.iDVenuse ?? eventRecord?.iDVenues;
10423: 
10424:   String? get distanceValue =>
10425:       eventData.hasDistance() ? eventData.distance.toString() : null;
10426: 
10427:   String get distanceLabel {
10428:     if (!eventData.hasDistance()) {
10429:       return 'ไม่ระบุ';
10430:     }
10431:     return '${eventData.distance.toStringAsFixed(1)} กม.';
10432:   }
10433: 
10434:   DateTime? get eventDate => eventRecord?.date ?? eventData.date;
10435: 
10436:   String get eventDateLabel {
10437:     final date = eventDate;
10438:     if (date == null) {
10439:       return 'ยังไม่ระบุวันเวลา';
10440:     }
10441: 
10442:     final day = functions.dateEventday(date)?.toString() ?? '';
10443:     final month = functions.dateMonthTH(date) ?? '';
10444:     final time = dateTimeFormat('Hm', date);
10445:     return '$day $month • $time';
10446:   }
10447: 
10448:   List<String> get artists {
10449:     final recordArtists = eventRecord?.nameArtise ?? const <String>[];
10450:     final source = recordArtists.isNotEmpty
10451:         ? recordArtists
10452:         : eventData.nameArtise;
10453:     return source.where((name) => name.trim().isNotEmpty).toList();
10454:   }
10455: 
10456:   String get eventTitle {
10457:     if (artists.isNotEmpty) {
10458:       return artists.join(', ');
10459:     }
10460:     return venueName;
10461:   }
10462: 
10463:   String get venueName {
10464:     final venueName = venueRecord?.nameVenuse.trim();
10465:     if (venueName != null && venueName.isNotEmpty) {
10466:       return venueName;
10467:     }
10468: 
10469:     final eventStore = eventRecord?.nameStore.trim();
10470:     if (eventStore != null && eventStore.isNotEmpty) {
10471:       return eventStore;
10472:     }
10473: 
10474:     final dataStore = eventData.nameStore.trim();
10475:     if (dataStore.isNotEmpty) {
10476:       return dataStore;
10477:     }
10478: 
10479:     return 'ร้านของ event นี้';
10480:   }
10481: 
10482:   String get venueImageUrl {
10483:     final logo = venueRecord?.logo.trim();
10484:     if (logo != null && logo.isNotEmpty) {
10485:       return _safeEventsImageUrl(logo, fallback: _kEventsFallbackProfileUrl);
10486:     }
10487: 
10488:     final bg = venueRecord?.bg.trim();
10489:     if (bg != null && bg.isNotEmpty) {
10490:       return _safeEventsImageUrl(bg, fallback: _kEventsFallbackProfileUrl);
10491:     }
10492: 
10493:     return _kEventsFallbackProfileUrl;
10494:   }
10495: 
10496:   String get posterUrl {
10497:     final poster = eventRecord?.poster.trim();
10498:     if (poster != null && poster.isNotEmpty) {
10499:       return _safeEventsImageUrl(poster, fallback: _kEventsFallbackPosterUrl);
10500:     }
10501: // MISSING LINE
10502: // MISSING LINE
10503: // MISSING LINE
10504: // MISSING LINE
10505: // MISSING LINE
10506: // MISSING LINE
10507: // MISSING LINE
10508: // MISSING LINE
10509: // MISSING LINE
10510: // MISSING LINE
10511: // MISSING LINE
10512: // MISSING LINE
10513: // MISSING LINE
10514: // MISSING LINE
10515: // MISSING LINE
10516: // MISSING LINE
10517: // MISSING LINE
10518: // MISSING LINE
10519: // MISSING LINE
10520:       return dataStyle;
10521:     }
10522: 
10523:     final venueStyles = venueRecord?.styleMusic ?? const <String>[];
10524:     return venueStyles.isNotEmpty ? venueStyles.join(', ') : 'ไม่ระบุ';
10525:   }
10526: 
10527:   String get detailText {
10528:     final detail = eventRecord?.detail.trim();
10529:     if (detail != null && detail.isNotEmpty) {
10530:       return detail;
10531:     }
10532:     return 'ยังไม่มีรายละเอียดเพิ่มเติมสำหรับ event นี้';
10533:   }
10534: 
10535:   String get openCloseTime {
10536:     final value = venueRecord?.openCloseTime.trim();
10537:     return value != null && value.isNotEmpty ? value : 'ยังไม่ระบุเวลาเปิด';
10538:   }
10539: 
10540:   String get ratingLabel {
10541:     final rating = venueRecord?.rating ?? 0.0;
10542:     return rating > 0 ? rating.toStringAsFixed(1) : 'ยังไม่มีคะแนน';
10543:   }
10544: 
10545:   String get doorsOpenLabel {
10546:     if (openCloseTime != 'ยังไม่ระบุเวลาเปิด') {
10547:       return openCloseTime;
10548:     }
10549: 
10550:     return 'Based on venue schedule';
10551:   }
10552: 
10553:   String get showStartsLabel {
10554:     final date = eventDate;
10555:     if (date == null) {
10556:       return 'Based on selection';
10557:     }
10558: 
10559:     return dateTimeFormat('Hm', date);
10560:   }
10561: 
10562:   List<String> get detailParagraphs {
10563:     final paragraphs = detailText
10564:         .split(RegExp(r'\n\s*\n|\n'))
10565:         .map((line) => line.trim())
10566:         .where((line) => line.isNotEmpty)
10567:         .toList();
10568: 
10569:     return paragraphs.isNotEmpty ? paragraphs : [detailText];
10570:   }
10571: 
10572:   List<String> get highlights {
10573:     final items = <String>[];
10574: 
10575:     if (artists.isNotEmpty) {
10576:       items.add('การแสดงโดย ${artists.join(', ')}');
10577:     }
10578:     if (musicStyle != 'ไม่ระบุ') {
10579:       items.add('แนวเพลง $musicStyle');
10580:     }
10581:     if (tableCount != null) {
10582:       items.add('มีโต๊ะสำหรับ event นี้ $tableCountLabel');
10583:     }
10584:     final max = maxCapacity;
10585:     if (max != null && max > 0) {
10586:       items.add('รองรับผู้ร่วมงานสูงสุด $maxCapacityLabel');
10587:     }
10588:     if (venueName != 'ร้านของ event นี้') {
10589:       items.add('จัดที่ $venueName');
10590:     }
10591: 
10592:     return items;
10593:   }
10594: 
10595:   String get maxCapacityLabel {
10596:     final max = maxCapacity;
10597:     if (max == null || max <= 0) {
10598:       return 'ไม่ระบุ';
10599:     }
10600:     return '$max คน';
10601:   }
10602: 
10603:   List<String> get importantItems {
10604:     return [
10605:       'จำนวนโต๊ะ ราคา และที่นั่งว่างอาจเปลี่ยนตามวันที่เลือก',
10606:       'เงื่อนไขการเข้า event และ dress code ขึ้นกับร้าน',
10607:       isFree
10608:           ? 'สิทธิ์ฟรีอาจมีจำนวนจำกัดหรือมีเงื่อนไขเพิ่มเติม'
10609:           : 'ราคาเริ่มต้นอาจยังไม่รวมเงื่อนไขอื่นของร้าน',
10610:     ];
10611:   }
10612: 
10613:   List<String> get tags {
10614:     final values = <String>[
10615:       musicStyle,
10616:       ...?eventRecord?.styleVenues,
10617:       ...?venueRecord?.styleVenuse,
10618:     ];
10619: 
10620:     final normalized = <String>[];
10621:     for (final value in values) {
10622:       final trimmed = value.trim();
10623:       if (trimmed.isNotEmpty &&
10624:           trimmed != 'ไม่ระบุ' &&
10625:           !normalized.contains(trimmed)) {
10626:         normalized.add(trimmed);
10627:       }
10628:     }
10629:     return normalized.take(6).toList();
10630:   }
10631: 
10632:   List<String> get venuePhotos {
10633:     final photos = <String>[];
10634:     for (final url in [...?venueRecord?.photos, ...?venueRecord?.promotion]) {
10635:       final trimmed = url.trim();
10636:       if (trimmed.isNotEmpty && !photos.contains(trimmed)) {
10637:         photos.add(trimmed);
10638:       }
10639:     }
10640:     return photos;
10641:   }
10642: 
10643:   LatLng? get venuePosition =>
10644:       eventRecord?.location ?? venueRecord?.position ?? eventData.position;
10645: 
10646:   String? get googleMapsUrl {
10647:     final position = venuePosition;
10648:     if (position == null) {
10649:       return null;
10650:     }
10651:     return 'https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1'
10652:         '&query=${position.latitude},${position.longitude}';
10653:   }
10654: 
10655:   String? get calendarUrl {
10656:     final date = eventDate;
10657:     if (date == null) {
10658:       return null;
10659:     }
10660:     final start = DateFormat("yyyyMMdd'T'HHmmss").format(date);
10661:     final end = DateFormat(
10662:       "yyyyMMdd'T'HHmmss",
10663:     ).format(date.add(const Duration(hours: 3)));
10664:     return Uri.https('calendar.google.com', '/calendar/render', {
10665:       'action': 'TEMPLATE',
10666:       'text': eventTitle,
10667:       'dates': '$start/$end',
10668:       'details': detailText,
10669:       'location': venueName,
10670:     }).toString();
10671:   }
10672: 
10673:   String get shareText =>
10674:       '$eventTitle • $venueName\n$eventDateLabel\n$posterUrl';
10675: 
10676:   List<_EventContactLink> get contactLinks {
10677:     if (venueRecord == null || !venueRecord!.hasLinkContact()) {
10678:       return const [];
10679:     }
10680:     final contact = venueRecord!.linkContact;
10681:     final links = <_EventContactLink>[];
10682: 
10683:     if (contact.ig.trim().isNotEmpty) {
10684:       links.add(
10685:         _EventContactLink(
10686:           icon: FontAwesomeIcons.instagram,
10687:           iconColor: const Color(0xFFE1306C),
10688:           label: '@${contact.ig.trim()}',
10689:           subtitle: 'Follow on Instagram',
10690:           url: functions.addsocial(
10691:             'instagram://user?username=',
10692:             contact.ig.trim(),
10693:           )!,
10694:         ),
10695:       );
10696:     }
10697:     if (contact.facebook.trim().isNotEmpty) {
10698:       links.add(
10699:         _EventContactLink(
10700:           icon: Icons.facebook_rounded,
10701:           iconColor: const Color(0xFF1877F2),
10702:           label: contact.facebook.trim(),
10703:           subtitle: 'Follow on Facebook',
10704:           url: functions.addsocial('fb://profile/', contact.facebook.trim())!,
10705:         ),
10706:       );
10707:     }
10708:     if (contact.line.trim().isNotEmpty) {
10709:       final lineUrl = functions.linkLine(contact.line.trim());
10710:       if (lineUrl != null) {
10711:         links.add(
10712:           _EventContactLink(
10713:             icon: FontAwesomeIcons.line,
10714:             iconColor: const Color(0xFF06C755),
10715:             label: contact.line.trim(),
10716:             subtitle: 'ติดต่อทาง LINE',
10717:             url: lineUrl,
10718:           ),
10719:         );
10720:       }
10721:     }
10722:     if (contact.tiktok.trim().isNotEmpty) {
10723:       links.add(
10724:         _EventContactLink(
10725:           icon: Icons.tiktok_rounded,
10726:           iconColor: Colors.white,
10727:           label: '@${contact.tiktok.trim()}',
10728:           subtitle: 'Follow on TikTok',
10729:           url: functions.addsocial(
10730:             'snssdk1233://user/',
10731:             contact.tiktok.trim(),
10732:           )!,
10733:         ),
10734:       );
10735:     }
10736:     if (contact.phone.trim().isNotEmpty) {
10737:       links.add(
10738:         _EventContactLink(
10739:           icon: Icons.call_rounded,
10740:           iconColor: const Color(0xFFFF3B3B),
10741:           label: contact.phone.trim(),
10742:           subtitle: 'โทรติดต่อร้าน',
10743:           url: functions.addsocial('tel:', contact.phone.trim())!,
10744:         ),
10745:       );
10746:     }
10747: 
10748:     return links;
10749:   }
10750: }

11490: 
11491: class _EventDetailHero extends StatefulWidget {
11492:   const _EventDetailHero({
11493:     required this.posterUrl,
11494:     required this.shareText,
11495:     required this.topSafeInset,
11496:   });
11497: 
11498:   final String posterUrl;
11499:   final String shareText;
11500:   final double topSafeInset;
11501: 
11502:   @override
11503:   State<_EventDetailHero> createState() => _EventDetailHeroState();
11504: }
11505: 
11506: class _EventDetailHeroState extends State<_EventDetailHero> {
11507:   double? _posterAspect;
11508:   ImageStream? _imageStream;
11509:   ImageStreamListener? _imageListener;
11510: 
11511:   @override
11512:   void didChangeDependencies() {
11513:     super.didChangeDependencies();
11514:     _resolvePosterAspect();
11515:   }
11516: 
11517:   @override
11518:   void didUpdateWidget(covariant _EventDetailHero oldWidget) {
11519:     super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
11520:     if (oldWidget.posterUrl != widget.posterUrl) {
11521:       _resolvePosterAspect();
11522:     }
11523:   }
11524: 
11525:   void _resolvePosterAspect() {
11526:     final listener = _imageListener;
11527:     if (listener != null) {
11528:       _imageStream?.removeListener(listener);
11529:     }
11530:     final stream = NetworkImage(
11531:       widget.posterUrl,
11532:     ).resolve(createLocalImageConfiguration(context));
11533:     final newListener = ImageStreamListener((info, _) {
11534:       if (!mounted) {
11535:         return;
11536:       }
11537:       setState(() {
11538:         _posterAspect = info.image.width / info.image.height;
11539:       });
11540:     }, onError: (error, stackTrace) {});
11541:     _imageStream = stream;
11542:     _imageListener = newListener;
11543:     stream.addListener(newListener);
11544:   }
11545: 
11546:   @override
11547:   void dispose() {
11548:     final listener = _imageListener;
11549:     if (listener != null) {
11550:       _imageStream?.removeListener(listener);
11551:     }
11552:     super.dispose();
11553:   }
11554: 
11555:   Widget _buildBackdrop() {
11556:     return Stack(
11557:       fit: StackFit.expand,
11558:       children: [
11559:         Image.network(
11560:           widget.posterUrl,
11561:           fit: BoxFit.cover,
11562:           color: const Color(0xAA000000),
11563:           colorBlendMode: BlendMode.darken,
11564:           errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => Container(
11565:             color: const Color(0xFF141414),
11566:             child: const Center(
11567:               child: Icon(
11568:                 Icons.event_rounded,
11569:                 color: Colors.white70,
11570:                 size: 54.0,
11571:               ),
11572:             ),
11573:           ),
11574:         ),
11575:         const DecoratedBox(
11576:           decoration: BoxDecoration(
11577:             gradient: LinearGradient(
11578:               colors: [Color(0xD9000000), Color(0x88000000), Color(0xF0000000)],
11579:               stops: [0.0, 0.58, 1.0],
11580:               begin: Alignment.topCenter,
11581:               end: Alignment.bottomCenter,
11582:             ),
11583:           ),
11584:         ),
11585:       ],
11586:     );
11587:   }
11588: 
11589:   Widget _buildPoster() {
11590:     return LayoutBuilder(
11591:       builder: (context, constraints) {
11592:         return Center(
11593:           child: ConstrainedBox(
11594:             constraints: BoxConstraints(
11595:               maxWidth: constraints.maxWidth,
11596:               maxHeight: constraints.maxHeight,
11597:             ),
11598:             child: DecoratedBox(
11599:               decoration: BoxDecoration(
11600:                 borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24.0),
11601:                 boxShadow: const [
11602:                   BoxShadow(
11603:                     blurRadius: 26.0,
11604:                     color: Color(0x99000000),
11605:                     offset: Offset(0.0, 14.0),
11606:                   ),
11607:                 ],
11608:               ),
11609:               child: ClipRRect(
11610:                 borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(24.0),
11611:                 child: Hero(
11612:                   tag: widget.posterUrl,
11613:                   child: Image.network(
11614:                     widget.posterUrl,
11615:                     fit: BoxFit.contain,
11616:                     filterQuality: FilterQuality.high,
11617:                     loadingBuilder: (context, child, loadingProgress) {
11618:                       if (loadingProgress == null) {
11619:                         return child;
11620:                       }
11621: 
11622:                       return SizedBox(
11623:                         width: constraints.maxWidth,
11624:                         height: constraints.maxHeight,
11625:                         child: const Center(
11626:                           child: CircularProgressIndicator(
11627:                             strokeWidth: 2.0,
11628:                             color: Colors.white,
11629:                           ),
11630:                         ),
11631:                       );
11632:                     },
11633:                     errorBuilder: (context, error, stackTrace) => SizedBox(
11634:                       width: constraints.maxWidth,
11635:                       height: constraints.maxHeight,
11636:                       child: Container(
11637:                         color: const Color(0xFF141414),
11638:                         child: const Center(
11639:                           child: Icon(
11640:                             Icons.event_rounded,
11641:                             color: Colors.white70,
11642:                             size: 54.0,
11643:                           ),
11644:                         ),
11645:                       ),
11646:                     ),
11647:                   ),
11648:                 ),
11649:               ),
11650:             ),
11651:           ),
11652:         );
11653:       },
11654:     );
11655:   }
11656: 
11657:   @override
11658:   Widget build(BuildContext context) {
11659:     final screenSize = MediaQuery.sizeOf(context);
11660:     final posterTopInset = widget.topSafeInset + 58.0;
11661:     const horizontalInset = 18.0;
11662:     const posterBottomInset = 16.0;
11663: 
11664:     final posterWidth = screenSize.width - (horizontalInset * 2);
11665:     final aspect = _posterAspect;
11666:     final posterHeight = aspect == null
11667:         ? screenSize.height * 0.42
11668:         : posterWidth / aspect;
11669: 
11670:     return AnimatedContainer(
11671:       duration: const Duration(milliseconds: 220),
11672:       curve: Curves.easeOut,
11673:       width: double.infinity,
11674:       height: posterTopInset + posterHeight + posterBottomInset,
11675:       child: Stack(
11676:         fit: StackFit.expand,
11677:         children: [
11678:           _buildBackdrop(),
11679:           Positioned.fill(
11680:             left: horizontalInset,
11681:             top: posterTopInset,
11682:             right: horizontalInset,
11683:             bottom: posterBottomInset,
11684:             child: _buildPoster(),
11685:           ),
11686:           Positioned(
11687:             top: widget.topSafeInset + 8.0,
11688:             left: 0.0,
11689:             right: 0.0,
11690:             child: Center(
11691:               child: Container(
11692:                 width: 44.0,
11693:                 height: 4.0,
11694:                 decoration: BoxDecoration(
11695:                   color: Colors.white54,
